    WHERE t."Id" = o0."OrderId") AS "ItemCount", CASE
    WHEN t."CancelledAt" IS NOT NULL THEN 'Cancelled'
    WHEN d."Status" = 'Completed' THEN 'Completed'
    ELSE 'Active'
END AS "Status"

    SELECT o."Id", o."CancelledAt", o."DiningSessionId", o."OrderNumber", o."PlacedAt"

    LIMIT @__p_2 OFFSET @__p_1
) AS t
